# Seed 83 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - nine hours, no gain: the stopping rule stops (Iteration 572)

`seed 83 9h then experiment review-checks.py spread then experiment appbench.py --zip ~/Downloads/appbench.zip --sample 16 then experiment carry-check.py --all` at f2f527a40. The chain: done. 32,400 designs, generations 0-126: 19,011 ok, 8,578 stopped after one round, 4,430 screened, 349 died: reach limit at scale 0 - not run, 28 wrong output: apps, 4 died: kernel workload; 3,600 designs an hour.

**The front**: the true one 180 designs - the report's 180, the same.

**The fixed-box hypervolume**: true front 180 (180 inside the box), hypervolume 0.33579 | fastest 0.1469, smallest 14187, least memory 112 KB, fastest at 32 bits 0.1799. **The stopping rule** (stopping.py, results/stopping-series.md): verdict: seed 83 (at its last generation, 126 - the earlier runs there too) gains nothing - the hypervolume 0.33579 against 0.34106 (-1.5%); the fastest 0.1459 against 0.1378 (+5.8%); the smallest 14,187 against 14,043 (+1.0%); the least memory 108 KB against 108 KB (+0 KB); the fastest at 32 bits 0.1782 against 0.1683 (+5.9%) the count: 7 run(s) on the method; STOP - three runs or more, the last two gaining nothing (if no correctness issue is open)

**The extremes**: fastest bce73d10ad 0.1469, smallest 3b83a0ccc7 14,187 bytes, least memory 112 KB, fastest at 32 bits 591259db08 0.1799.

**One round first**: 8,578 children stopped after one round, 30.8% of those measured; 946 audited in full, 0 of them on the front.

**The operators**: borrowed 38.8%, crossover 30.1%, mutation 31.1% of the children; the last fifth borrowed 39.7%, crossover 25.3%, mutation 35.0%; the front's entries per child 0.558% (180 of 32,250).

**The JIT designs**: 42 of the front, 18 of its fastest tenth's 18; jitmul 19, jitfuse 8, jitexec 4, jitos 0, jitrp 0.

**The fast end re-measured** (remeasure.json, 86 designs): the fastest ten 1.073 re-measured / in-run, the JIT designs 1.057, the rest 0.998.

**The experiments**: the spread at generation 126 - | build/evolve/db.jsonl | 126 | 19011 | 291 | 0.1459 | 14,187 | 108 | 9.9351e+05 | 0.6071 | 0.1931 |; appbench - **Spearman, in-run speed against the applications' geometric mean: 0.918 over 16 designs.**; the clock test - none; truefronts - none.

**The remote checks** (results/xcheck-seed83.md): dream.lan-prebuilt 24 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.989; dream.lan-prebuilt32 30 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.932; dream.lan-prebuilt64 30 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.993; rage.lan-native 6 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.143.

**Held out** (heldout.py): sieve (held out): 180 designs; Spearman with the selection speed 0.959 at the top, where selection chooses: the fastest third (60) 0.811, the fastest tenth (18) 0.907 regret: the selection's fastest, bce73d10ad, 0.068 held out; the held-out fastest, bce73d10ad, 0.068 - the pick +0.0%

**The curse** (curse.txt - rule 3, 0.98-1.05 works): fastest tenth    7 designs: median 1.089, range 1.059-1.107 fastest third   24 designs: median 1.059, range 1.016-1.107

**Progress**: the smallest binary + image (exact) gained in 25 generations; the last at generation 116 of 126; in the last third 2 gains, 27 bytes in all the best speed gained 2% or more in 16 generations; the last at generation 46 (24: 5.1%, 25: 4.8%, 30: 3.3%, 40: 2.6%, 46: 2.6%)

**The votes**: all four this run: scale, sharedcall, msc, rtimm, noinls, jitdoes, jitcfa, jitrp, jitos, alignhot, jitvar, regpin over the last 3 runs in the ledger (81, 82, 83): candidates - scale (12 of 12), sharedcall (12 of 12), msc (12 of 12), jitdoes (12 of 12), jitrp (12 of 12), jitos (12 of 12), alignhot (12 of 12), jitvar (12 of 12); no keep vote and half the votes or more - none

**The freezes lifted** (lifted.py): prediction 556 (the freezes were right; values as built): HOLDS - bytehdr, thinhdr, bss, lean off 1 on 5%- : yes; alignhot, msc, sharedcall each on under 10%: yes; doesfar and varcall off 1 nowhere: yes

**At 32 bits** (fail32.txt): at 32 bits: 19993 designs measured, 22 failed expected, no fault: 22 of 22 (cpt16's reach at 4-byte cells - Iteration 517); not a known cause: 0


**The deaths**: 28 wrong output: apps, 349 died: reach limit at scale 0 - not run, 4 died: kernel workload. deaths-check.py rebuilt them here (design, generation, in the run, genes, here): none to rebuild.

**The chain on master**: process-checks.sh at 9664214: build 0, run-tests PASS, validate 7 of 7 IDENTICAL, the gate alive 194 of 194, ans-check 0 of 48 differ (its negative control caught), deaths-check: 0 of 0 rebuilt compute right here; 0 open, the jail PASS.

**The verdict**: by the stopping rule (stopping.py) seed 83 **gains nothing** at its last generation, 126 - the
hypervolume 0.33579 against 0.34106 (-1.5%); the fastest 0.1459 against 0.1378 (+5.8%), the smallest 14,187 against
14,043 (+1.0%), the memory 108 KB against 108, the fastest at 32 bits 0.1782 against 0.1683 (+5.9%): no measure better.
At equal effort (the spread, generation 126, seeds 79-83) the slowest at the fast end (the others 0.1378-0.1444) and the
third smallest (14,043-14,364); its best speed last gained 2% at generation 46. Its fastest tenth re-measures 1.089 of
in-run (seeds 78-82: 1.048-1.094). The deaths all expected (deaths-check: cpt16 on apps 28, not run at the reach limit
349, the scale-1 reach 4): no open correctness issue. **The rule stops**: seven runs on the method (77-83), the last two
gaining nothing - the improvement phase ends; next the confirmation run (GOALS.md step 2: seed 84, 12 hours - the owner -
widths carryall). **Its founders**: carry-check.py --all in this chain - 5,320 designs from seeds 79-83, the sample 40 of
40 right against s6 - found none of carry-named.txt's five: tools/clean-runs.sh keeps the newest four archives (their
databases in the seed packs), so carryall would have carried seeds 79-83 alone; lab/evolve/restore-archives.sh (572)
brings seeds 71-78 back from their packs first. **Predictions**: 569's hold whole - no gain and the stop; the fastest at
126, 0.1459, at or above 0.1378; carry-check 40 of 40.

# Evolved VM designs

27970 designs evaluated, 19011 alive, 4430 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann, apps,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 3b5e1162dd, speed 0.342, binary + image 20,594, memory 140 KB - Q 0.300 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
